और राजपत्रित अधिसूचना की प्रतियां जनता को तारीख 
6 फरवरी, 1992 को उपलब्ध करा वो गई थो ; 


और उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) 
के अनुमण मे मक्षम प्राधिकारी ने केन्द्रीय सरकार को रिपोर्ट 


New Delhi, the 19th Novomber, 1992 
S . O . 3031.- Wheres by the notifiction of the Governmentof 
India in theMinistry of Petroleum and NaturalGas, No . S . O . 
135 , dated the 20th Deceniber, 1991 issued under sub - section 
( 1 ) of section 3 of the Petroleum and Minerals Pipelines (AC 
quisition of Right of User in LAnd ) Act, 1962 ( 40 of 1962 ) 
( hereinaferreferred to as the said Act.) , the central Govern 
ment declared its intention to acquire the right of user in the 
lands specified in the Schedule appended to that notica 
tion for the purpose of laying pipeline for the transport of 
petrolcum : 

And whereas the Copiesof the said Gazette notification were 
made available to the public on 6th February, 1992 : 

And wheras the Competent Authority in pursuance of sub . 
section ( 1 ) of section 6 of the said Act has made his report to 
the Central Goverjiment; 


और केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने 
के पश्चात इम अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट 
भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन करने का विनिश्चय 
किया है । 

अतः अब , केन्द्रीय सरकार, उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 
की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए 
यह घोषणा करती है कि इस अधिसुचना में मलग्न अनसूची 
में विनिदिष्ट भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार अजित किए जाते 


And wher as the Central Governmenthas, after consider 
ing tho said report, decided to acquire the rightof usci in the 
landsspecified in the Schedule apponded to this notification ; 


Now , therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub 
section (1) of section 6 of the said Act, the CentralGovornment 
hereby declares that the right of user in the lands specified in 
the Schedule appended to this notification are hereby 
acquired ; And further in exercise of thepowersconferred by sub 
section (4 ) of section 6 the ContralGovernment hereby directs 
that the right of user in the said lands shall instead of vesting 
in the Central Government, vest in the Indian Oil Corporation 
Limited free from all cncumbrances, 


यह और कि केन्द्रीय सरकार धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 4 ) 
सारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हए , यह निदेश देती 
है कि उक्त भूमि में उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार 
में निहित होने की बजाए मभी विल्लंगमों से मक्त इडियन 
पायल कापोरेशन लिमिटेड में निहित होगा ।

6 . The most important Jucent relied upon by the mail 
agement is the photo copy of Formu B Register . It may be 
noted that Form B Register is a statutory register to be 
maintained under Section 18 of the Minos, Act, 1962 and 
cvery cietails of the individual workmun including the age or 
the date of birth is noted at the time of initial appointment 
and the siguature or the LTI of the workmen is obtained in 
Coken of the correctness of the entry . Thus the job of the 
clerk noting the details in the register is very important and 
responsible because a workman is supposed in retire on the 
basis of the age recorded in the register. Ext. M - 1 is the 
photo copy of Form B Register filed by the management 
wherein his age has been shown as 33 years on 20 - 2 -61. 
The concetned workman had also signed at the appropriate 
place . This means he was born in the year 1928 but this

4 . La course of hearing it is urged by the managemcat

ployment. In this view of the matter , my finding would bu

1. This is a reference under Section 10 ( 1 ) ( d ) of the Indust 
rial Disputcs Act, 1947 made by the Central Government 
for adjudication of the disputc vide Order No. L - 26011 / 2 / 80

is as follows ; - . 
" Whether denial of employnient to S /Shri Dhaban Nark , 

Kishan Munda and Bhudev Bapli , Drillers by the

2 . The case of the workpien , namely , Bhuban Naik , Shri 
Kishan Muntla and Bhudev Bapti, Drillers is that thoy were 
working under the management since last theo years (1,0 . 
from 1977 ) . They demanded revision of their wages rato and 
for othor facilitics as applicable to tho establishment. The 
management without considering their grievances denied them 
work with effect from 15th March , 1979 without any written 
order . They represented to the higher authority of the 
management against the illegal action of the Manager, When 
no action was taken , they raised tho proseat dispute. The 
managenient did not attend the conciliation proceedings on 
the ground that they would not sit with the unrecognised 
union representatives . The management have signed an agroo 
merit on 11 - 5 - 1978 with the Koonjhar Mincs and Forest 
Workers Union that they will pay mininum wage rate of

the workmen demanded higher wages, the management took 
the aforesaid illegal action against the workmen , 


that the three workmen were the drillers of the First party 
management and they were disengaged by the first party .

in the new Colony where the workmen revide . Bhudey Bapti 
was also residing in the same colony. This witocys bus scen 
hiin going for work . Nothing has been clicited from this 
witness to discard his evidence . Ilic witncy No . 5 who was

piirty and when they uenianded enhancement of their wages 
di perta : nent workers, employment was refused to them . It 
appears from his cross -exanination that the place of work 
of these three workmen was at distance of about 50 ycars 
from his place of work . It appears that he has heard the 
Manager telling the workers not to come for work any 
further I do not find anything in the evidence of this witness 
10 rejcct his evidence. The solo witness for the management 
Gays that the claim of the three workmen thut ibey were 
drillers under the first party is false . In cross - examination 
this witness admits that he can not say as to who were the 
employees serving under the first party during his tenure of 
service i. c . from April , 1976 to December , 1979 and from 
November , 1980 to October , 1984 . He has admitted in his 
cro9s- examination that the witness No. 4 for the workmen 
was a private tutor and that the management was paying somo 
thing on weekly basis for his tcaching to thic childron of the 
colony. His oral evidence that these workmen were not the 
employees under the monacement can not under any circums 
tances out weigh the evidence of the five witnesses for the

evidence therefore, ono can come to the uphesitating conclu 
sion that the three workmen were under the employment of

3 . The management s stand is that the order of reference 
made at the instance of the North Orissa Workers Union is not 
maintainable . This Union is a foreign union and is not a 
representative union of the Company s workers. The work 
men in question were at no point of tmc in the employment 
of the management and the question of refusal of their employ 
ment by the denigement at any time doey not prise . In the 
carly part of the year 1979 when the North Orissa Workers

said union had at no point of time been in their employment. 
The other allegations Dado in the written statement by the 
worknien have been denied . 


5. Regarding ibc documentary evidence as indicated earlier 
the alleged agreement betweeu the workman Bhuban Naik and 
the management would only he suggestive of the conclusion 
that he was a workman under the management. As stated by 
tie three workmen the management had not issued any appoint 
munt letter to them . It appears that the management s 
offlcials were recording their daily attendance . It was up to 
the manugement to produce tho attendance register for the 
iclevant period to show that they were never tho workmen 
under the management. The witness for the management 
has stated that their company was maintaining B form register 
in respect of all the workers of the company and that register 
would show as to who were the workers under the company 
and for which period . The management does not choose to 
prove those documents. The payment voucher if any of the 
workman during tho relevant period must also be in possession 
of the management. There appears to have no reason why 
tle management fights slly in producing those documents . 
An adverse inference has to be drawn against the manage 
nient that had it produced those registers they could have gone 
against it. On a consideration of tho entire material, I om 
inclined to held that the three workmen were under the 
employment of the company , In view of this finding the 
clain of the workmen that they were refused employment 
must be accepted as truo. 


4 . The first question that arises for consideration in this 
case is whether the thrce workmen were in employment of 
the management. The workmen in support of their claim of

6 . The next question is as to the maintainability of the 
reforence . Accordnig to the management the three work non

The dispute is relating to the refusal of employment to the

2 . Tnc workman namely Mohabir Iwly and Mohan Tant 
claim to have been the Ore Checkers under the Management. 
Their casc is that they were working under the Managenient 
as buch since the ycar 1974 . They demanded revision of their 
wage rate and for other facilities us applicable to the establish 
meut. The Management without considering their gricvanccs 
denicd theni work with effect from 3 - 10- 1979. They rcprcsent 
ed the higher authority of the Management against the illegal 
action of the Manager . When no action was taken, they 
raiscal the present dispute . The Management did not attend 
the conciliation procecdings on the ground that they would 
not sit with the unrecognised union representatives, The 
Labour Enforcement Officer ( Central) , Barbil enquired 
into the employment and wage rates of the workmen on 
2 - 12 - 1979 in presence of the witnesses and found that they 
were working for 4 - 1 / 2 years and were getting Rs. 4 / - per 
day . The copy of the cnquiry report has been enclosed as 
annexure - 1. 


witnesses namely Madhu Munda and one Maninohan Singh , 
the attendance clerk . This document does not as such prove 
ibat Mahabir Tanti was a workman under the Management, 
In view of the evidence of the two workmen and the two 
note books as also the challan book it was for the Manage 
inent to adduce sufficient rebuttal evidence to suggest that 
the two workmen never worked under the Management, Ono 
CX -Manager of the Company has denicd the issue of Exts . 1 . 
2 and 3 by the Company. From the very entries made in 
Exts. 1 , 2 and 3 this mero denial by the ex -manager that 
they were never issued by the Company is not acceptable . 
This wilness in his cross-cxamination has admitted that B 
jegister maintained by the Company would sbow as to who 
wore its workers . That register has not been produced . No 
olhe: register or record is also produced by the Company 
to ncgative the claim of the workmen that they were under 
the cmployment of the Company. On a consideration of 
both oral and documentary evidence adduced by the parties 
I an inclined to hold that the two workmen were under tho 
cmployment of the Company. 


5 . Both the workmen have stated that they were refused 
Empoyment by the Company . It is the case of the Manaye 
ment that the question of refusal of employment docs not 
arise in as much as the workmen were never under its emp 
loyment. Having held that the workmen were under the 
employment of the Company it must be held that iho Com 
pany refused employment as alleged . 


6 . The next question is about the majnta mablity of tho 
rcverence. The case of the Management is that the North 
Orissi Workers Union is a forcign Union and is not the 
represcatative of the workmen . It is also said to be a Union 
not l . cognised by the Company. On behalf of the workmen 
no evidence las been adduced to show that these two work 
men were also members of the North Orissa Workers Union . 
It can not therefore be said that the Union could have espou 
sed their cause . But the fact remains thut this is a disputo 
as mentioned in the reference between the employer and the 
workmcn . As provided in Section 2 ( a ) of the Industrial 
Dispuitos Act , the workmen in case of denial of employment 
could raise their dispute individually and as such the dispute 
Can not be said to be not an industrial dispute . The Iofe 
rence is therefore maintainable . 


7 . While considering the question of relief it is scen that 
the parties have neither pleaded nor adduced any evidence 
as to the gainful employment of the workmen during the 
period in quostion . I would , therefore, before finally dispos 
ing the matter call upon the partics to adduce evidence on 
the question of gainful cmploymcot. Inform both the 
partics. 

R . N . PANDA , Presiding Officer . 


3 . The Management s stand is that the order of reference 
made at the instance of the North Orissa Workers Union 
is not maintainable . This Union is a forcign Union and is 
not a representative Union of the Coinpany s Workers. The 
workmen in question were at no point of time in the emp. 
loyment of the Management and the question of refusal of 
their employment by the Management at any time does not 
urise. In the carly part of the year 1979 when the North 
Orissa Worker s Union had raised the disputc with the Ma 
nagement, the Management vide its letter dated 19th Octo 
ber , 1979 had categorically stated and denied that the 
workers named by the said Union had al no point of time 
been in their employment. The other allegations matie in 
the written statement by the workmen have been denied.

6 . I have heard hoth tho parties and gone through the 
record . The rep . of the management has laid stresses that 
Kendriya Vidyalaya is an educational institution and this does 
not fail within the definition of industry and as a result the 
the petitioner is not workman and the provisions of the I. D . 

let 250 not attractive . Tie contention is mcritless . The 
inatter was already considered in Bangalore Water Supply and 
Sewerage Board Vs . A , Kajappa 1978 LIC . 467 wherein it 
has been held that in the educational institutions there are 
number of other uctivities than teaching which inay be sevc 
rable from the teaching activities and these operations viewed 
separately ne collectively , hy themselves , inay be treated as an 
industry . Siinilar view has been talen in Sri Jagannath 
Simiskrit Visivilyaları VS , Srihu Belera and others reported 
in 1989 ( 2 ) LLN 242 wherein it has been held that the educa 
tonal justitutions is 1911 industry in so far is it relates to the 
killed or unskilled worker s but the teachers can not be treated 
4 Wohinau . Thus the position boils down the situation that 
cducational 1113titutions may assume double charactor, the 

unturion of which will cercnt on the facts and evidence 
u vil casc . Jdmittedly the petitioner had no concern with 
the teaching aspect of the Rail institution and falls in the

AWARD 
The Govt. of fndia , Ministry of Labour in excr 
cise of the powers conferred on them under Seciion 
10 ( 1 ) ( d ) of the 1. D . Act., 1947 has referred the 
following dispute to this Tribunal for adjudication 
vide their Order No. L - 24012 ( 85 ) 87 -D .IV ( B ) . 
dated , the 23rd December, 1987 . 

SCHEDULE

is justified ? If so , to what relief these
